# Remodel 100: Gemini file, part 2 (1180 firms)

This continues `GEMINI_COMPLETE.md`. Rows 1 to 120 are done; this file has the other 1180 firms, with the SAME row
numbers (so they are not in number order). The firms most focused on kitchens and baths come first, so the first
replies are the most useful.

Each firm already passes our other checks. What is missing is proof of recent activity: TWO different kinds of
activity dated 2026-07-16 to 2026-10-14 (for example a Google review in that window plus a Facebook post in that window).

## How to use it
1. Upload this whole file to Gemini.
2. Paste the prompt below as your first message.
3. Gemini answers 40 rows at a time. Type `continue` for the next 40, and keep going.
4. Paste the answers here (as you have been doing). Partial results are fine.

## Prompt to paste into Gemini

```
You are collecting public facts about US kitchen and bath remodeling companies. Use Google Maps and Google Search.
The attached file has a table of companies. Work through the table IN THE ORDER LISTED (row numbers are not in
order; keep each company's own row number), 40 companies per reply. When I type "continue", do the next 40.

For each company, find:
1. Google reviews: open its Google Maps listing (maps_url), sort reviews by Newest, take the newest review.
2. Facebook: use ONLY the page in the facebook_linked_from_site column. Give that profile URL exactly and the date
   of its latest post. If the column says "none linked", write UNKNOWN for all three Facebook fields.
3. Instagram: use ONLY the account in the instagram_linked_from_site column. Give that profile URL and the date of
   its latest post. If the column says "none linked", write UNKNOWN for both Instagram fields.
4. Job posting: any job ad for this company (its own careers page, Indeed, ZipRecruiter, LinkedIn Jobs) WITH the
   date it was posted. No date means UNKNOWN.
5. Owner: a person named on the company's OWN WEBSITE with a role of owner, co-owner, founder, president, CEO or
   principal, with full first and last name. Give the exact page URL where the name and role appear together.
6. Email: an email address shown on the company's OWN WEBSITE. Give the exact page URL. Never guess an email.

Strict rules:
- Never guess, estimate or invent anything. If you cannot see a value, write UNKNOWN.
- Dates must be YYYY-MM-DD. If only a relative date is shown ("3 weeks ago"), write it exactly as shown.
- Copy text exactly. Keep each copied text to 15 words or fewer.
- Write plain URLs only. Never format anything as a link, never use [text](url), never wrap URLs in google.com/search.
- Give profile URLs (facebook.com/name, instagram.com/handle), never a post or reel link.
- Write "closed" in status if Google shows the business as permanently or temporarily closed.

Reply ONLY with CSV rows (no commentary), using exactly this header in your first reply and no header after that:
row,business_name,checked_on,status,newest_review_date,newest_review_first_words,review_count,rating,facebook_url,facebook_latest_post_date,facebook_post_first_words,instagram_url,instagram_latest_post_date,job_posting_url,job_posting_date,job_title,owner_name,owner_role,owner_source_url,email,email_source_url

checked_on is today's date (YYYY-MM-DD). Put any text that contains a comma inside double quotes.
```

## What I do with the answers
- Review, Facebook and Instagram dates are recorded as supplied by you through Gemini.
- Owner names and emails are used only as leads; an agent opens the firm's own page and quotes it before anything counts.
